ZF=OT(Ot(ZF,ZQ),7)Zi=((Zi+Zh))%Oe ZT=OT(Ot(ZT,Zi),7)end local function Zb(ZB,Zj,ZG,ZH,Zn,Za)if#ZB==0 then return ZB end ZG,ZH,Zn,Za=ZG or 1,ZH or 0,Zn or 0,Za or 0 local Zg,wy={},1 while wy<=#ZB do local wk=ZO(Zj,ZG,ZH,Zn,Za)for ws=1,16,1 do local wO=wk[ws]for wZ=1,0x4,1 do if wy>#ZB then break end Zg[wy]=OI(Ot(OY(ZB,wy),wO%0X100))wO,wy=OX(wO/0X100),wy+1 end end ZG=((ZG+1))%Oe end return Oz(Zg)end local function ww(wL,wK,wM)return ZO(wL,0,wK,wM%Oe,OX(wM/Oe))end local function wW(wr,wv,wN,wV,wA)local wf=ww(wA,wN,wV)local wd={Ot(wv,wf[1])}for wU=1,8,1 do wd[wU+1]=Ot(wr[wU],wf[wU+1])end wd[0Xa]=wf[0xa]return wd end local function wo(wR,wq,wu,wD)local wX=ww(wD,wq,wu)return{Ot(wR,wX[1]),wX[10]}end local function wY(wI,wz,we,wp,wt)local wT=ww(wt,we,wp)return wI[2]==wT[0xa]and wI[1]==Ot(wz,wT[1])end local function wF(wC,wJ,wc,wi,wQ)local wS=ww(wQ,wc,wi)if wC[10]~=wS[0Xa]or wC[1]~=Ot(wJ,wS[1])then return nil end local wm={}for wl=1,8,1 do wm[wl]=Ot(wC[wl+1],wS[wl+1])end return wm end local function wh(wP)local wb={}for wB=1,#wP,1 do local wj=wP[wB]wb[wB]=OI(wj%256,OX(wj/0x100)%256,OX(wj/65536)%0X100,OX(wj/kd[0X7])%256)end return Oz(wb)end return{block=ZO,crypt=Zb,seal=wW;sealAdmission=wo;admit=wY,resolve=wF;packWords=wh}end))()local wH={kd[8];kd[0X9],kd[10],kd[11],kd[12];kd[0xd],kd[0Xe];kd[15]}local function wn(wa,Ly,Lk)local Ls=(kV(143808444))local LO={}local LZ=kA[kV(0xa665f89)][kV(0x50f953c4)]for Lw=1,#Ls,1 do LO[LZ(Ls,Lw)]=Lw-1 end local LL=kA[kV(0Xa665f89)][kV(558074266)]local LK=kA[kV(177354831)][kV(0xe430315)]local kA[kV(0X4b32dff6)](wa)~=(kV(174481289))then return wa end local LV=LL(wa)local LA={}local Lf=1 while Lf<=LV do local Ld=(LV-Lf)+1 local LU=Ld>=5 and 5 or Ld local Lo=0 local LR=LU>1 for Lq=0,0x4,1 do local Lu if Lq<LU then Lu=LO[LZ(wa,Lf+Lq)]if not Lu then LR=false break end else Lu=0X54 end Lo=Lo*85+Lu end if LR then local LD=LK(Lo/kd[0x7])%0X100 local LX=LK(Lo/0x10000)%0X100 local LY=LK(Lo/0X100)%0X100 local LI=Lo%0x100 if LU==0X5 then LW(LA,LM(LD,LX,LY,LI))elseif LU==4 then LW(LA,LM(LD,LX,LY))elseif LU==3 then LW(LA,LM(LD,LX))elseif LU==0x2 then LW(LA,LM(LD))end end Lf=Lf+LU end return Lv(Lr(LA))end local Lz={}local KZ=((function(Le,Lp,Lt,LT)local LF=wn(Lt,LT)local LC,LJ=kA[kV(0Xa665f89)][kV(0x50f953c4)],kA[kV(0xa665f89)][kV(984429579)]local function Lc(Li,LQ)local LS,Lm,Ll,Lh=LC(Li,LQ,LQ+0x3)return((LS+Lm*256)+Ll*0x10000)+Lh*kd[0x7]end local LP={}local Lb,LB=1,1 for LG=1,#LF,4 do local LH=Lc(LF,LG)local Ln=LH%0X10000 local La=kA[kV(177354831)][kV(0xe430315)](LH/65536)LP[Ln]={Lb;La;LB}Lb,LB=Lb+La,LB+1 end LF=nil return function(Lg)local Ky=LP[Lg]if not Ky then return nil end local
